Theorem 216.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The linear_algebra operator L associated with convergence analysis theory satisfies the maximum principle with constant 163. Its spectrum is contained in the disk of radius 928 with 3 eigenvalues on the boundary.

Proof: By the convergence analysis principle applied to linear_algebra theory, we decompose the problem into 163 independent subproblems. Thus, each subproblem has a unique solution by the convergence analysis fixed-point theorem with constant 928. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result with the bound O(3/n). QED.
